# Plan: Integrate K-Search + R-Zero + Karpathy Autoresearch into Kernel Optimization Pipeline

## Context

The Luma AMD Speedrun (deadline March 30, 2026) has an autonomous kernel optimization loop at
`research/challenges/luma_amd_speedrun/autoresearch/`. Three external references need proper
integration:

| Reference | What It Is | Current Integration | Gap |
|-----------|-----------|-------------------|-----|
| K-Search (arxiv 2602.19128) | LLM-guided tree search with co-evolving world model for GPU kernels | 60% — tree structure, K=7 stagnation, priority selection implemented | Missing: LLM pi_plan (INSERT/UPDATE/PRUNE), LLM pi_code (code synthesis), V-score co-evolution |
| R-Zero (2508.05004) | Challenger-Solver co-evolution for LLM training | Conceptual only — `r_zero.py` tracks metrics, no kernel application | Missing: Challenger that proposes harder optimization targets, difficulty calibration |
| Karpathy autoresearch | Autonomous 5-min experiment loops with LLM code modification | 80% — overnight loop, fixed metric, convergence detection, crash recovery | Missing: LLM modifies code directly instead of templates; program.md research strategy layer |

**Core gap across all three: our autoresearch uses deterministic templates + parameter perturbation,
not LLM-guided code synthesis. This is the single biggest upgrade.**

## Approach: Three-Layer Enhancement

### Layer 1: K-Search World Model (pi_plan) — Highest Impact

**What:** Replace static pre-seeded trees with LLM-driven INSERT/UPDATE/PRUNE operations.

**Files to modify:**
- `autoresearch/ksearch_tree.py` — Add `insert_child()`, `update_v_scores()`, `strategic_prune()` methods
- `autoresearch/analyzer.py` — After analyzing results, call LLM to propose new child strategies
- `autoresearch/driver.py` — Add world model evolution step after each cycle

**Implementation:**
```python
# In analyzer.py — after analyzing results:
def evolve_world_model(tree, node, result, llm_client):
    """K-Search Phase 3: World model co-evolution via LLM."""
    trajectory = tree.get_trajectory(node.id)  # All attempts on this branch

    prompt = f"""You are a GPU kernel optimization world model.

    Current tree state: {tree.to_summary()}
    Just tested: {node.strategy} → {result.geomean_us}µs (baseline: {result.baseline_us}µs)
    Trajectory: {trajectory}

    Based on this result, propose:
    1. INSERT: New child strategies worth exploring (0-2 children)
    2. UPDATE: Revised V-scores for affected nodes
    3. PRUNE: Branches that should be abandoned

    Output JSON: {{"insert": [...], "update": {...}, "prune": [...]}}"""

    response = llm_client.generate(prompt)
    tree.apply_evolution(response)  # INSERT/UPDATE/PRUNE atomically
```

**LLM choice:** Local Ollama (qwen3-coder:30b) — free, fast enough for 1 call per 8-min cycle.
No API costs. Falls back to current deterministic behavior if Ollama unavailable.

### Layer 2: K-Search Code Instantiation (pi_code) — Medium Impact

**What:** Replace template-based generator with LLM-guided code synthesis.

**Files to modify:**
- `autoresearch/generator.py` — Add LLM code generation path alongside templates
- New: `autoresearch/code_synthesizer.py` — LLM-based submission.py generation

**Implementation:**
```python
# In code_synthesizer.py:
def synthesize_kernel(strategy, kernel_type, reference_code, constraints, llm_client):
    """K-Search pi_code: Generate kernel implementation from strategy description."""
    prompt = f"""Generate a submission.py for AMD MI355X {kernel_type} kernel.

    Strategy: {strategy}
    Reference implementation: {reference_code}
    Constraints: {constraints}  # Dead ends, blocked APIs, etc.

    The submission must:
    1. Define custom_kernel(data: input_t) -> output_t
    2. Pass correctness within rtol=1e-2
    3. Be faster than baseline

    Output ONLY the Python code."""

    code = llm_client.generate(prompt)
    if validate_syntax(code):
        return code
    return None  # Fall back to template generator
```

**Key insight from K-Search paper:** pi_code samples implementations REPEATEDLY until K=7
stagnation. Each sample is a new LLM generation with the same strategy but different code.
This separates "strategy failure" from "implementation failure."

### Layer 3: R-Zero Challenger Pattern — Low Impact, Long-Term

**What:** Add difficulty calibration to the optimization loop. The Challenger proposes
*harder* optimization targets (tighter performance thresholds, new shape combinations)
to push the Solver beyond local optima.

**Files to modify:**
- `autoresearch/driver.py` — Add challenger step that adjusts target thresholds
- `src/cohezion/optimization/r_zero.py` — Connect to kernel optimization metrics

**Implementation:**
```python
# In driver.py — after convergence detected:
def challenge_plateau(tree, kernel, current_best):
    """R-Zero: Propose harder target when plateau detected."""
    # Instead of stopping at convergence, raise the bar
    new_target = current_best * 0.9  # 10% harder
    tree.insert_child(
        parent_id="root",
        strategy=f"Beat {new_target:.1f}µs (10% below plateau)",
        priority=0.8,
    )
```

**This is lightweight** — just adjusts targets rather than generating adversarial problems.
The full R-Zero Challenger-Solver loop is unnecessary for kernel optimization since we don't
control the problem difficulty (competition shapes are fixed).

### Layer 4: Karpathy program.md Pattern — Quick Win

**What:** Create a `research_strategy.md` file that guides the LLM agent's optimization
direction. Human-editable research strategy that the LLM reads before each cycle.

**Files to create:**
- `autoresearch/research_strategy.md` — Current optimization priorities, known dead ends, focus areas

**Implementation:**
```markdown
# Research Strategy (Human-Editable)

## Current Focus
- MoE: Try direct moe_sorting_fwd with local_expert_mask parameter
- GEMM: Explore tritonblas.matmul_fp4 as alternative to gemm_a4w4
- MLA: At Python ceiling, deprioritize

## Dead Ends (Do NOT retry)
- [loaded from kernel-specific skills automatically]

## Budget Allocation
- MoE: 50% (closest to leader, new untested path)
- GEMM: 30% (1.45x gap, limited paths)
- MLA: 20% (15.8x gap, Python ceiling)
```

The LLM world model reads this before proposing strategies, ensuring human research direction
guides the autonomous loop.

## Critical Files

| File | Action | Purpose |
|------|--------|---------|
| `autoresearch/ksearch_tree.py` | Modify | Add `insert_child()`, `apply_evolution()`, trajectory tracking |
| `autoresearch/analyzer.py` | Modify | Add `evolve_world_model()` LLM call after each analysis |
| `autoresearch/driver.py` | Modify | Add world model evolution step, challenger pattern |
| `autoresearch/generator.py` | Modify | Add LLM fallback alongside templates |
| `autoresearch/code_synthesizer.py` | Create | LLM-based kernel code generation |
| `autoresearch/research_strategy.md` | Create | Karpathy-style human research direction |
| `~/.claude/skills/k-search-llm-kernel-optimization/SKILL.md` | Modify | Update with implementation status |

## Task Sequence

1. **[ ] Add trajectory tracking to KNode** — Store full attempt history, not just best_result
2. **[ ] Implement `insert_child()` and `apply_evolution()`** in ksearch_tree.py
3. **[ ] Create code_synthesizer.py** with Ollama integration (qwen3-coder:30b)
4. **[ ] Add world model evolution step** to analyzer.py (call LLM after each result)
5. **[ ] Create research_strategy.md** with current priorities from kernel skills
6. **[ ] Add challenger pattern** to driver.py (raise target on plateau)
7. **[ ] Wire generator.py** to try LLM synthesis first, fall back to templates
8. **[ ] Dry-run test** — Run 3 cycles with LLM evolution, verify tree evolves sensibly
9. **[ ] Update K-Search skill** with implementation status

## Verification

1. **Dry-run:** `python autoresearch/driver.py --dry-run --cycles 3` — verify LLM world model
   proposes sensible INSERT/UPDATE/PRUNE operations
2. **Template fallback:** Disable Ollama, verify system falls back to deterministic templates
3. **Tree evolution:** After 5 cycles, verify tree has new nodes that weren't in the pre-seed
4. **Code synthesis:** Verify at least 1 LLM-generated submission passes correctness test
5. **Research strategy:** Verify LLM reads research_strategy.md and respects dead ends

## Risk Mitigation

- **Ollama unavailable:** Fall back to template generation (current behavior)
- **LLM generates bad code:** Syntax validation + correctness test gate before benchmark
- **LLM proposes dead ends:** Feed kernel-specific skill dead-end lists into system prompt
- **Budget overrun:** Cap at 1 LLM call per cycle (~8min), max 4096 tokens per call

```
     1→"""Result analysis → K-Search tree updates.
     2→
     3→Analyzes benchmark results to compute priority updates,
     4→identify bottleneck shapes, and suggest next experiments.
     5→"""
     6→
     7→from __future__ import annotations
     8→
     9→import json
    10→import math
    11→from dataclasses import dataclass
    12→from pathlib import Path
    13→from typing import Any
    14→
    15→from evaluator import EvalResult
    16→from ksearch_tree import KSearchTree, KNode
    17→
    18→
    19→@dataclass
    20→class Analysis:
    21→    """Analysis of a benchmark result."""
    22→    geomean_us: float
    23→    bottleneck_shape: str | None  # Shape contributing most to geomean
    24→    improvement_pct: float  # vs previous best (negative = regression)
    25→    per_shape_rank: dict[str, int]  # Rank of each shape (1 = worst)
    26→    suggestion: str  # Next experiment recommendation
    27→
    28→
    29→def compute_geomean(timings: list[float]) -> float:
    30→    """Geometric mean of timing values."""
    31→    positive = [t for t in timings if t > 0]
    32→    if not positive:
    33→        return float("inf")
    34→    log_sum = sum(math.log(t) for t in positive)
    35→    return math.exp(log_sum / len(positive))
    36→
    37→
    38→def analyze_result(
    39→    result: EvalResult,
    40→    tree: KSearchTree,
    41→    node: KNode,
    42→) -> Analysis:
    43→    """Analyze a benchmark result and update the K-Search tree.
    44→
    45→    Returns Analysis with insights and auto-updates the tree.
    46→    """
    47→    if not result.success:
    48→        tree.mark_failed(node.id, result.error)
    49→        return Analysis(
    50→            geomean_us=float("inf"),
    51→            bottleneck_shape=None,
    52→            improvement_pct=0.0,
    53→            per_shape_rank={},
    54→            suggestion=f"Node failed: {result.error}. Try different parameters.",
    55→        )
    56→
    57→    geomean = result.geomean_us or float("inf")
    58→
    59→    # Update tree with result
    60→    tree.update_result(node.id, geomean)
    61→
    62→    # Find bottleneck shape (highest absolute time)
    63→    bottleneck = None
    64→    per_shape_rank: dict[str, int] = {}
    65→    if result.per_shape_us:
    66→        sorted_shapes = sorted(
    67→            result.per_shape_us.items(), key=lambda x: x[1], reverse=True
    68→        )
    69→        per_shape_rank = {shape: i + 1 for i, (shape, _) in enumerate(sorted_shapes)}
    70→        bottleneck = sorted_shapes[0][0] if sorted_shapes else None
    71→
    72→    # Compute improvement vs tree's best
    73→    stats = tree.get_stats()
    74→    prev_best = stats.get("best_us")
    75→    improvement = 0.0
    76→    if prev_best and prev_best > 0:
    77→        improvement = (prev_best - geomean) / prev_best * 100
    78→
    79→    # Generate suggestion
    80→    if geomean == float("inf"):
    81→        suggestion = "No valid timing. Check correctness."
    82→    elif bottleneck and result.per_shape_us:
    83→        worst_time = result.per_shape_us[bottleneck]
    84→        suggestion = (
    85→            f"Focus on shape {bottleneck} ({worst_time:.1f}µs) — "
    86→            f"it's the bottleneck. Try varying parameters for this shape."
    87→        )
    88→    else:
    89→        suggestion = "Try broader parameter sweep."
    90→
    91→    return Analysis(
    92→        geomean_us=geomean,
    93→        bottleneck_shape=bottleneck,
    94→        improvement_pct=improvement,
    95→        per_shape_rank=per_shape_rank,
    96→        suggestion=suggestion,
    97→    )
    98→
    99→
   100→def log_result(
   101→    result: EvalResult,
   102→    node: KNode,
   103→    analysis: Analysis,
   104→    log_path: Path,
   105→) -> None:
   106→    """Append result to JSONL log file."""
   107→    entry = {
   108→        "node_id": node.id,
   109→        "strategy": node.strategy,
   110→        "parameters": node.parameters,
   111→        **result.to_dict(),
   112→        "analysis": {
   113→            "geomean_us": analysis.geomean_us,
   114→            "bottleneck_shape": analysis.bottleneck_shape,
   115→            "improvement_pct": analysis.improvement_pct,
   116→            "suggestion": analysis.suggestion,
   117→        },
   118→    }
   119→    log_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
   120→    with open(log_path, "a") as f:
   121→        f.write(json.dumps(entry) + "\n")
   122→
   123→
   124→def load_results(log_path: Path) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
   125→    """Load all results from JSONL log."""
   126→    if not log_path.exists():
   127→        return []
   128→    results = []
   129→    for line in log_path.read_text().strip().split("\n"):
   130→        if line:
   131→            results.append(json.loads(line))
   132→    return results
   133→
   134→
   135→def get_best_result(log_path: Path) -> dict[str, Any] | None:
   136→    """Get the best result (lowest geomean) from log."""
   137→    results = load_results(log_path)
   138→    successful = [r for r in results if r.get("success") and r.get("geomean_us")]
   139→    if not successful:
   140→        return None
   141→    return min(successful, key=lambda r: r["geomean_us"])
   142→
```

```
     1→"""Template-based submission.py generator.
     2→
     3→Takes a KNode's parameters + template, produces a valid submission.py.
     4→Uses string.Template $PARAM substitution to avoid {/} escaping issues.
     5→"""
     6→
     7→from __future__ import annotations
     8→
     9→import ast
    10→import json
    11→import re
    12→from pathlib import Path
    13→from string import Template
    14→from typing import Any
    15→
    16→from templates import moe_template, gemm_template, gemm_cached_template, mla_template
    17→
    18→TEMPLATES = {
    19→    "moe": moe_template.TEMPLATE,
    20→    "gemm": gemm_template.TEMPLATE,
    21→    "gemm_cached": gemm_cached_template.TEMPLATE,
    22→    "mla": mla_template.TEMPLATE,
    23→}
    24→
    25→DEFAULTS = {
    26→    "moe": moe_template.DEFAULT_PARAMS,
    27→    "gemm": gemm_template.DEFAULT_PARAMS,
    28→    "gemm_cached": gemm_cached_template.DEFAULT_PARAMS,
    29→    "mla": mla_template.DEFAULT_PARAMS,
    30→}
    31→
    32→
    33→def generate_submission(
    34→    kernel: str,
    35→    parameters: dict[str, Any],
    36→    output_path: Path | None = None,
    37→) -> str:
    38→    """Generate a submission.py from template + parameters.
    39→
    40→    Args:
    41→        kernel: "moe", "gemm", or "mla"
    42→        parameters: Overrides for template defaults
    43→        output_path: If provided, write the generated code to this file
    44→
    45→    Returns:
    46→        Generated Python source code
    47→    """
    48→    if kernel not in TEMPLATES:
    49→        raise ValueError(f"Unknown kernel: {kernel}. Expected: {list(TEMPLATES)}")
    50→
    51→    # Merge defaults with overrides
    52→    params = {**DEFAULTS[kernel], **parameters}
    53→
    54→    # Convert Python objects to repr strings for template substitution
    55→    subs = {}
    56→    for key, value in params.items():
    57→        if isinstance(value, (dict, list)):
    58→            subs[key] = json.dumps(value)
    59→        elif isinstance(value, bool):
    60→            subs[key] = str(value)
    61→        elif isinstance(value, (int, float)):
    62→            subs[key] = str(value)
    63→        else:
    64→            subs[key] = str(value)
    65→
    66→    template = Template(TEMPLATES[kernel])
    67→    code = template.substitute(subs)
    68→
    69→    # Check for unresolved template variables (catches typos)
    70→    unresolved = re.findall(r"\$[A-Z_]+", code)
    71→    if unresolved:
    72→        raise ValueError(f"Unresolved template variables: {unresolved}")
    73→
    74→    # Validate syntax
    75→    try:
    76→        ast.parse(code)
    77→    except SyntaxError as e:
    78→        raise ValueError(f"Generated code has syntax error: {e}\n---\n{code}") from e
    79→
    80→    if output_path:
    81→        output_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    82→        output_path.write_text(code)
    83→
    84→    return code
    85→
    86→
    87→def generate_from_node(
    88→    kernel: str,
    89→    node_parameters: dict[str, Any],
    90→    output_dir: Path,
    91→) -> Path:
    92→    """Generate submission.py from a K-Search node's parameters.
    93→
    94→    Returns path to generated file.
    95→    """
    96→    output_path = output_dir / "submission.py"
    97→    generate_submission(kernel, node_parameters, output_path)
    98→    return output_path
    99→
```

```
     1→#!/usr/bin/env python3
     2→"""Autonomous K-Search experiment driver.
     3→
     4→Main overnight loop:
     5→  SELECT node → generate submission → popcorn-cli test → benchmark
     6→  → UPDATE tree → log JSONL → rotate kernels → repeat
     7→
     8→Usage:
     9→  uv run python driver.py                    # Run all kernels (priority-weighted)
    10→  uv run python driver.py --kernel moe       # Run only MoE experiments
    11→  uv run python driver.py --kernel gemm      # Run only GEMM experiments
    12→  uv run python driver.py --kernel mla       # Run only MLA experiments
    13→  uv run python driver.py --max-cycles 10    # Limit number of cycles
    14→  uv run python driver.py --dry-run          # Generate submissions but don't submit
    15→"""
    16→
    17→from __future__ import annotations
    18→
    19→import argparse
    20→import json
    21→import logging
    22→import random
    23→import shutil
    24→import sys
    25→import time
    26→from datetime import datetime
    27→from pathlib import Path
    28→
    29→# Add autoresearch to path
    30→sys.path.insert(0, str(Path(__file__).parent))
    31→
    32→from ksearch_tree import KSearchTree
    33→from generator import generate_submission
    34→from evaluator import evaluate, test_then_benchmark, EvalResult, KERNEL_DIRS
    35→from analyzer import analyze_result, log_result, Analysis
    36→from rate_limiter import RateLimiter
    37→
    38→logging.basicConfig(
    39→    level=logging.INFO,
    40→    format="%(asctime)s [%(levelname)s] %(message)s",
    41→    datefmt="%H:%M:%S",
    42→)
    43→log = logging.getLogger("autoresearch")
    44→
    45→BASE_DIR = Path(__file__).parent
    46→TREE_DIR = BASE_DIR / "tree"
    47→RESULTS_DIR = BASE_DIR / "results"
    48→KERNELS_DIR = BASE_DIR.parent / "kernels"
    49→
    50→# Priority weights for kernel rotation (higher = more likely to be selected)
    51→# Based on gap-to-leader: MoE 1.27x (closeable), GEMM 2.4x, MLA 15.6x
    52→KERNEL_WEIGHTS = {
    53→    "moe": 0.5,   # Closest to leader — highest ROI
    54→    "gemm": 0.3,  # Medium gap
    55→    "mla": 0.2,   # Largest gap — moonshot
    56→}
    57→
    58→CYCLE_SLEEP_SECONDS = 5  # Sleep between cycles
    59→
    60→
    61→def load_tree(kernel: str) -> KSearchTree:
    62→    """Load or create K-Search tree for a kernel."""
    63→    tree_path = TREE_DIR / f"{kernel}_tree.json"
    64→    if tree_path.exists():
    65→        return KSearchTree.load(tree_path)
    66→    tree = KSearchTree(kernel)
    67→    tree.insert_root(f"Optimize {kernel} geomean")
    68→    return tree
    69→
    70→
    71→def save_tree(tree: KSearchTree) -> None:
    72→    """Persist tree to disk."""
    73→    tree_path = TREE_DIR / f"{tree.kernel_name}_tree.json"
    74→    tree.save(tree_path)
    75→
    76→
    77→def select_kernel(kernels: list[str]) -> str:
    78→    """Weighted random selection of next kernel to optimize."""
    79→    if len(kernels) == 1:
    80→        return kernels[0]
    81→    weights = [KERNEL_WEIGHTS.get(k, 0.33) for k in kernels]
    82→    total = sum(weights)
    83→    weights = [w / total for w in weights]
    84→    return random.choices(kernels, weights=weights, k=1)[0]
    85→
    86→
    87→def generate_variant_params(tree: KSearchTree, node) -> dict:
    88→    """Generate a parameter variant from the node.
    89→
    90→    If the node has been attempted before, slightly perturb parameters
    91→    to explore nearby configurations.
    92→    """
    93→    params = dict(node.parameters)
    94→
    95→    if node.attempts == 0:
    96→        return params  # First attempt uses exact parameters
    97→
    98→    # Perturbation: modify one random value in a lookup table
    99→    kernel = tree.kernel_name
   100→
   101→    if kernel == "moe" and "KSPLIT_TABLE" in params:
   102→        table = dict(params["KSPLIT_TABLE"])
   103→        if table:
   104→            key = random.choice(list(table.keys()))
   105→            current = table[key]
   106→            # Perturb KSPLIT by ±1-2, clamped to 0-6
   107→            delta = random.choice([-2, -1, 1, 2])
   108→            table[key] = max(0, min(6, current + delta))
   109→            params["KSPLIT_TABLE"] = table
   110→
   111→    elif kernel == "gemm" and "KERNEL_TABLE" in params:
   112→        table = dict(params["KERNEL_TABLE"])
   113→        if table:
   114→            key = random.choice(list(table.keys()))
   115→            entry = dict(table[key])
   116→            # Perturb log2_ks by ±1, clamped to 0-4
   117→            delta = random.choice([-1, 1])
   118→            entry["log2_ks"] = max(0, min(4, entry["log2_ks"] + delta))
   119→            # Occasionally try ASM variant
   120→            if random.random() < 0.3:
   121→                entry["kernel"] = random.choice(["gemm_a4w4", "gemm_a4w4_asm"])
   122→            table[key] = entry
   123→            params["KERNEL_TABLE"] = table
   124→
   125→    elif kernel == "mla" and "SPLITS_TABLE" in params:
   126→        table = dict(params["SPLITS_TABLE"])
   127→        if table:
   128→            key = random.choice(list(table.keys()))
   129→            current = table[key]
   130→            # Perturb splits: ×2, ÷2, ±4
   131→            choices = [
   132→                max(2, current // 2),
   133→                min(64, current * 2),
   134→                max(2, current - 4),
   135→                min(64, current + 4),
   136→            ]
   137→            table[key] = random.choice(choices)
   138→            params["SPLITS_TABLE"] = table
   139→
   140→    return params
   141→
   142→
   143→def run_cycle(
   144→    kernel: str,
   145→    tree: KSearchTree,
   146→    rate_limiter: RateLimiter,
   147→    dry_run: bool = False,
   148→) -> tuple[bool, str]:
   149→    """Run one experiment cycle for a kernel.
   150→
   151→    Returns (success, summary_message).
   152→    """
   153→    # Select best node
   154→    node = tree.select_best()
   155→    if node is None:
   156→        return False, f"{kernel}: No active nodes remaining"
   157→
   158→    log.info(f"[{kernel}] Selected: {node.strategy} (p={node.priority:.2f}, attempts={node.attempts})")
   159→
   160→    # Generate variant parameters
   161→    params = generate_variant_params(tree, node)
   162→
   163→    # Generate submission (nodes can override template via _template param)
   164→    template_key = params.pop("_template", kernel)
   165→    try:
   166→        code = generate_submission(template_key, params)
   167→    except ValueError as e:
   168→        log.error(f"[{kernel}] Generation failed: {e}")
   169→        tree.mark_failed(node.id, str(e))
   170→        save_tree(tree)
   171→        return False, f"{kernel}: Generation failed: {e}"
   172→
   173→    if dry_run:
   174→        log.info(f"[{kernel}] DRY RUN — would submit {len(code)} chars")
   175→        return True, f"{kernel}: Dry run OK ({len(code)} chars)"
   176→
   177→    # Write submission to kernel directory
   178→    kernel_dir = KERNELS_DIR / KERNEL_DIRS[kernel]
   179→    staging_dir = kernel_dir / "staging"
   180→    staging_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
   181→
   182→    # Save to staging with timestamp
   183→    ts = datetime.now().strftime("%Y%m%d_%H%M%S")
   184→    staged_path = staging_dir / f"submission.autoresearch.{ts}.py"
   185→    staged_path.write_text(code)
   186→
   187→    # Copy to submission.py for popcorn-cli
   188→    submission_path = kernel_dir / "submission.py"
   189→    # Back up current submission
   190→    backup_path = staging_dir / f"submission.backup.{ts}.py"
   191→    if submission_path.exists():
   192→        shutil.copy2(submission_path, backup_path)
   193→    submission_path.write_text(code)
   194→
   195→    # Test first, then benchmark
   196→    log.info(f"[{kernel}] Testing...")
   197→    test_result = evaluate(submission_path, kernel, mode="test")
   198→
   199→    if not test_result.success:
   200→        log.warning(f"[{kernel}] Test FAILED: {test_result.error}")
   201→        tree.mark_failed(node.id, f"Test failed: {test_result.error}")
   202→        # Restore backup
   203→        if backup_path.exists():
   204→            shutil.copy2(backup_path, submission_path)
   205→        save_tree(tree)
   206→        # Log failure
   207→        log_result(
   208→            test_result, node,
   209→            Analysis(float("inf"), None, 0.0, {}, test_result.error),
   210→            RESULTS_DIR / f"{kernel}_runs.jsonl",
   211→        )
   212→        return False, f"{kernel}: Test failed: {test_result.error}"
   213→
   214→    log.info(f"[{kernel}] Test PASSED. Benchmarking...")
   215→    bench_result = evaluate(submission_path, kernel, mode="benchmark")
   216→
   217→    if not bench_result.success:
   218→        log.warning(f"[{kernel}] Benchmark FAILED: {bench_result.error}")
   219→        # Still update tree (test passed, benchmark failed is different from correctness failure)
   220→        if backup_path.exists():
   221→            shutil.copy2(backup_path, submission_path)
   222→        save_tree(tree)
   223→        return False, f"{kernel}: Benchmark failed: {bench_result.error}"
   224→
   225→    # Analyze and update tree
   226→    analysis = analyze_result(bench_result, tree, node)
   227→    save_tree(tree)
   228→
   229→    # Log result
   230→    log_result(bench_result, node, analysis, RESULTS_DIR / f"{kernel}_runs.jsonl")
   231→
   232→    geomean = bench_result.geomean_us or 0
   233→    log.info(
   234→        f"[{kernel}] Geomean: {geomean:.1f}µs "
   235→        f"(improvement: {analysis.improvement_pct:+.1f}%) "
   236→        f"Bottleneck: {analysis.bottleneck_shape}"
   237→    )
   238→
   239→    # If this is the best result AND leaderboard is available, consider submitting
   240→    stats = tree.get_stats()
   241→    if (
   242→        stats["best_us"] == geomean
   243→        and analysis.improvement_pct > 0
   244→        and rate_limiter.can_submit(kernel)
   245→    ):
   246→        log.info(f"[{kernel}] NEW BEST! Submitting to leaderboard...")
   247→        lb_result = evaluate(submission_path, kernel, mode="leaderboard")
   248→        if lb_result.success:
   249→            rate_limiter.record_submission(kernel)
   250→            log.info(f"[{kernel}] Leaderboard submission OK!")
   251→        else:
   252→            log.warning(f"[{kernel}] Leaderboard submission failed: {lb_result.error}")
   253→    else:
   254→        # Restore backup if not best
   255→        if backup_path.exists() and stats["best_us"] != geomean:
   256→            shutil.copy2(backup_path, submission_path)
   257→
   258→    summary = (
   259→        f"{kernel}: {geomean:.1f}µs "
   260→        f"(best={stats['best_us']:.1f}µs, "
   261→        f"{stats['active']} active nodes, "
   262→        f"{stats['total_attempts']} total attempts)"
   263→    )
   264→    return True, summary
   265→
   266→
   267→def main():
   268→    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description="K-Search autonomous experiment driver")
   269→    parser.add_argument("--kernel", choices=["moe", "gemm", "mla"], help="Single kernel to optimize")
   270→    parser.add_argument("--max-cycles", type=int, default=0, help="Max experiment cycles (0=unlimited)")
   271→    parser.add_argument("--dry-run", action="store_true", help="Generate but don't submit")
   272→    args = parser.parse_args()
   273→
   274→    kernels = [args.kernel] if args.kernel else ["moe", "gemm", "mla"]
   275→    rate_limiter = RateLimiter()
   276→
   277→    log.info(f"Starting K-Search driver for: {', '.join(kernels)}")
   278→    log.info(f"Max cycles: {'unlimited' if args.max_cycles == 0 else args.max_cycles}")
   279→    log.info(f"Rate limiter: {rate_limiter.status()}")
   280→
   281→    # Load trees
   282→    trees = {k: load_tree(k) for k in kernels}
   283→    for k, t in trees.items():
   284→        log.info(f"  {k}: {t}")
   285→
   286→    cycle = 0
   287→    # Convergence tracking: {kernel: (best_us_at_check, cycles_since_improvement)}
   288→    convergence: dict[str, tuple[float, int]] = {}
   289→    converged_kernels: set[str] = set()
   290→
   291→    try:
   292→        while args.max_cycles == 0 or cycle < args.max_cycles:
   293→            cycle += 1
   294→            # Skip converged kernels
   295→            active_kernels = [k for k in kernels if k not in converged_kernels]
   296→            if not active_kernels:
   297→                log.info("All kernels converged. Stopping.")
   298→                break
   299→            kernel = select_kernel(active_kernels)
   300→            tree = trees[kernel]
   301→
   302→            log.info(f"\n{'='*60}")
   303→            log.info(f"Cycle {cycle}: {kernel}")
   304→            log.info(f"{'='*60}")
   305→
   306→            success, summary = run_cycle(kernel, tree, rate_limiter, dry_run=args.dry_run)
   307→            log.info(summary)
   308→
   309→            # Periodic priority decay to encourage exploration
   310→            if cycle % 5 == 0:
   311→                for t in trees.values():
   312→                    t.decay_priorities(factor=0.95)
   313→                    save_tree(t)
   314→
   315→            # Convergence check every 10 cycles per kernel
   316→            if cycle % 10 == 0:
   317→                for k in active_kernels:
   318→                    stats = trees[k].get_stats()
   319→                    best = stats.get("best_us")
   320→                    if best is None:
   321→                        continue
   322→                    prev_best, stale_count = convergence.get(k, (float("inf"), 0))
   323→                    if prev_best > 0 and (prev_best - best) / prev_best < 0.01:
   324→                        stale_count += 1
   325→                    else:
   326→                        stale_count = 0
   327→                    convergence[k] = (best, stale_count)
   328→                    if stale_count >= 1:  # No >1% improvement in last 10 cycles
   329→                        converged_kernels.add(k)
   330→                        log.info(
   331→                            f"[{k}] CONVERGED at {best:.1f}µs "
   332→                            f"(no >1% improvement in 10 cycles). Reallocating."
   333→                        )
   334→
   335→            time.sleep(CYCLE_SLEEP_SECONDS)
   336→
   337→    except KeyboardInterrupt:
   338→        log.info("\nShutting down...")
   339→    finally:
   340→        # Save all trees
   341→        for t in trees.values():
   342→            save_tree(t)
   343→        # Print final stats
   344→        log.info(f"\n{'='*60}")
   345→        log.info(f"Final stats after {cycle} cycles:")
   346→        for k, t in trees.items():
   347→            stats = t.get_stats()
   348→            log.info(
   349→                f"  {k}: best={stats['best_us']}µs, "
   350→                f"active={stats['active']}, "
   351→                f"attempts={stats['total_attempts']}"
   352→            )
   353→        log.info(f"{'='*60}")
   354→
   355→
   356→if __name__ == "__main__":
   357→    main()
   358→
```

```
     1→"""K-Search tree for kernel optimization.
     2→
     3→Tree-structured search over kernel parameter configurations.
     4→Each node is a strategy (e.g., "KSPLIT=4 for shape X") with priority,
     5→attempt tracking, and stagnation-based pruning (K=7).
     6→"""
     7→
     8→from __future__ import annotations
     9→
    10→import json
    11→import uuid
    12→from dataclasses import dataclass, field, asdict
    13→from pathlib import Path
    14→from typing import Any
    15→
    16→
    17→STAGNATION_THRESHOLD = 7  # Prune after 7 attempts without improvement
    18→
    19→
    20→@dataclass
    21→class KNode:
    22→    id: str
    23→    strategy: str
    24→    parameters: dict[str, Any] = field(default_factory=dict)
    25→    parent_id: str | None = None
    26→    priority: float = 0.5  # 0.0 (low) to 1.0 (high)
    27→    attempts: int = 0
    28→    best_result_us: float | None = None
    29→    stagnation_count: int = 0
    30→    status: str = "active"  # active | exhausted | pruned
    31→    children: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    32→    notes: str = ""
    33→
    34→    def is_selectable(self) -> bool:
    35→        return self.status == "active"
    36→
    37→
    38→class KSearchTree:
    39→    """K-Search optimization tree with SELECT/INSERT/UPDATE/PRUNE."""
    40→
    41→    def __init__(self, kernel_name: str):
    42→        self.kernel_name = kernel_name
    43→        self.nodes: dict[str, KNode] = {}
    44→        self.root_id: str | None = None
    45→
    46→    def insert_root(self, strategy: str, parameters: dict | None = None) -> KNode:
    47→        node = KNode(
    48→            id=f"root_{self.kernel_name}",
    49→            strategy=strategy,
    50→            parameters=parameters or {},
    51→            priority=1.0,
    52→        )
    53→        self.nodes[node.id] = node
    54→        self.root_id = node.id
    55→        return node
    56→
    57→    def insert_child(
    58→        self,
    59→        parent_id: str,
    60→        strategy: str,
    61→        parameters: dict[str, Any],
    62→        priority: float = 0.5,
    63→        notes: str = "",
    64→    ) -> KNode:
    65→        if parent_id not in self.nodes:
    66→            raise KeyError(f"Parent {parent_id} not found")
    67→        node_id = f"n_{uuid.uuid4().hex[:8]}"
    68→        node = KNode(
    69→            id=node_id,
    70→            strategy=strategy,
    71→            parameters=parameters,
    72→            parent_id=parent_id,
    73→            priority=priority,
    74→            notes=notes,
    75→        )
    76→        self.nodes[node_id] = node
    77→        self.nodes[parent_id].children.append(node_id)
    78→        return node
    79→
    80→    def select_best(self) -> KNode | None:
    81→        """Select highest-priority active node (leaf-first, then internal)."""
    82→        active = [n for n in self.nodes.values() if n.is_selectable()]
    83→        if not active:
    84→            return None
    85→        # Prefer leaves (no active children) over internal nodes
    86→        _dummy = KNode(id="", strategy="", status="pruned")
    87→        leaves = [n for n in active if not any(
    88→            self.nodes.get(c, _dummy).is_selectable() for c in n.children
    89→        )]
    90→        candidates = leaves if leaves else active
    91→        return max(candidates, key=lambda n: n.priority)
    92→
    93→    def update_result(self, node_id: str, result_us: float) -> None:
    94→        """Update node with benchmark result. Tracks improvement/stagnation."""
    95→        node = self.nodes[node_id]
    96→        node.attempts += 1
    97→
    98→        if node.best_result_us is None or result_us < node.best_result_us:
    99→            improvement = (
   100→                (node.best_result_us - result_us) / node.best_result_us
   101→                if node.best_result_us
   102→                else 1.0
   103→            )
   104→            node.best_result_us = result_us
   105→            node.stagnation_count = 0
   106→            # Boost priority on improvement
   107→            node.priority = min(1.0, node.priority + 0.1 * improvement)
   108→        else:
   109→            node.stagnation_count += 1
   110→
   111→        # Auto-prune on stagnation
   112→        if node.stagnation_count >= STAGNATION_THRESHOLD:
   113→            node.status = "exhausted"
   114→            node.notes += f" [Exhausted after {node.attempts} attempts]"
   115→
   116→    def mark_failed(self, node_id: str, reason: str) -> None:
   117→        """Mark node as pruned due to failure (correctness, crash, etc.)."""
   118→        node = self.nodes[node_id]
   119→        node.status = "pruned"
   120→        node.notes += f" [PRUNED: {reason}]"
   121→
   122→    def decay_priorities(self, factor: float = 0.95) -> None:
   123→        """Decay all active node priorities to encourage exploration."""
   124→        for node in self.nodes.values():
   125→            if node.is_selectable():
   126→                node.priority *= factor
   127→
   128→    def get_stats(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
   129→        """Summary statistics for the tree."""
   130→        active = [n for n in self.nodes.values() if n.status == "active"]
   131→        exhausted = [n for n in self.nodes.values() if n.status == "exhausted"]
   132→        pruned = [n for n in self.nodes.values() if n.status == "pruned"]
   133→        best = min(
   134→            (n for n in self.nodes.values() if n.best_result_us is not None),
   135→            key=lambda n: n.best_result_us,  # type: ignore[arg-type]
   136→            default=None,
   137→        )
   138→        return {
   139→            "kernel": self.kernel_name,
   140→            "total_nodes": len(self.nodes),
   141→            "active": len(active),
   142→            "exhausted": len(exhausted),
   143→            "pruned": len(pruned),
   144→            "best_us": best.best_result_us if best else None,
   145→            "best_strategy": best.strategy if best else None,
   146→            "total_attempts": sum(n.attempts for n in self.nodes.values()),
   147→        }
   148→
   149→    # --- Persistence ---
   150→
   151→    def save(self, path: Path) -> None:
   152→        data = {
   153→            "kernel_name": self.kernel_name,
   154→            "root_id": self.root_id,
   155→            "nodes": {nid: asdict(n) for nid, n in self.nodes.items()},
   156→        }
   157→        path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
   158→        # Atomic write: write to tmp, then rename (prevents corruption on crash)
   159→        tmp = path.with_suffix(".tmp")
   160→        tmp.write_text(json.dumps(data, indent=2))
   161→        tmp.rename(path)
   162→
   163→    @classmethod
   164→    def load(cls, path: Path) -> KSearchTree:
   165→        data = json.loads(path.read_text())
   166→        tree = cls(data["kernel_name"])
   167→        tree.root_id = data["root_id"]
   168→        for nid, ndata in data["nodes"].items():
   169→            tree.nodes[nid] = KNode(**ndata)
   170→        return tree
   171→
   172→    def __repr__(self) -> str:
   173→        stats = self.get_stats()
   174→        return (
   175→            f"KSearchTree({self.kernel_name}: "
   176→            f"{stats['active']} active, {stats['total_attempts']} attempts, "
   177→            f"best={stats['best_us']}µs)"
   178→        )
   179→
```

> AGENT

Good, I have a solid understanding of the codebase. Let me now create the tasks and start implementing.

`★ Insight ─────────────────────────────────────`
The existing autoresearch loop follows a clean **SELECT → GENERATE → EVALUATE → UPDATE** cycle. The key gap is that `generate_submission()` uses deterministic `string.Template` substitution — it fills in parameter tables but can't invent new code. The K-Search paper's core insight is separating **pi_plan** (what strategy to try) from **pi_code** (how to implement it), allowing the LLM to operate at both levels. This decoupling is what lets K-Search escape local optima that parameter perturbation can't.
`─────────────────────────────────────────────────`

`★ Insight ─────────────────────────────────────`
**Trajectory tracking** is essential for the K-Search world model. Without it, the LLM only sees the best result per node. With full attempt histories, the LLM can distinguish "this strategy was tried 5 times and always gets ~200µs" (fundamentally limited) from "this strategy got 200µs once but also 150µs" (worth retrying with better code). This is the difference between **strategy failure** and **implementation failure** — the key K-Search insight.
`─────────────────────────────────────────────────`

```json
  "new_string": "\"\"\"K-Search tree for kernel optimization.\n\nTree-structured search over kernel parameter configurations.\nEach node is a strategy (e.g., \"KSPLIT=4 for shape X\") with priority,\nattempt tracking, stagnation-based pruning (K=7), and trajectory history\nfor LLM world model co-evolution.\n\"\"\"\n\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport json\nimport logging\nimport uuid\nfrom dataclasses import dataclass, field, asdict\nfrom pathlib import Path\nfrom typing import Any\n\n\nlog = logging.getLogger(\"ksearch_tree\")\n\nSTAGNATION_THRESHOLD = 7  # Prune after 7 attempts without improvement\n\n\n@dataclass\nclass AttemptRecord:\n    \"\"\"Single attempt on a node — used for trajectory tracking.\"\"\"\n    result_us: float\n    parameters: dict[str, Any] = field(default_factory=dict)\n    timestamp: str = \"\"\n    source: str = \"template\"  # \"template\" or \"llm\"\n\n\n@dataclass\nclass KNode:\n    id: str\n    strategy: str\n    parameters: dict[str, Any] = field(default_factory=dict)\n    parent_id: str | None = None\n    priority: float = 0.5  # 0.0 (low) to 1.0 (high)\n    attempts: int = 0\n    best_result_us: float | None = None\n    stagnation_count: int = 0\n    status: str = \"active\"  # active | exhausted | pruned\n    children: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)\n    notes: str = \"\"\n    attempt_history: list[dict[str, Any]] = field(default_factory=list)\n\n    def is_selectable(self) -> bool:\n        return self.status == \"active\"\n\n    def record_attempt(\n        self,\n        result_us: float,\n        parameters: dict[str, Any] | None = None,\n        source: str = \"template\",\n    ) -> None:\n        \"\"\"Record a single attempt for trajectory tracking.\"\"\"\n        from datetime import datetime\n        self.attempt_history.append({\n            \"result_us\": result_us,\n            \"parameters\": parameters or self.parameters,\n            \"timestamp\": datetime.now().isoformat(),\n            \"source\": source,\n        })\n\n    def get_trajectory_summary(self) -> str:\n        \"\"\"Compact trajectory for LLM prompts.\"\"\"\n        if not self.attempt_history:\n            return \"No attempts yet\"\n        results = [a[\"result_us\"] for a in self.attempt_history]\n        sources = [a.get(\"source\", \"template\") for a in self.attempt_history]\n        llm_count = sources.count(\"llm\")\n        return (\n            f\"{len(results)} attempts: \"\n            f\"min={min(results):.1f}µs, max={max(results):.1f}µs, \"\n            f\"last={results[-1]:.1f}µs, \"\n            f\"llm_generated={llm_count}/{len(results)}\"\n        )"
}
```

```json
{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/home/mike-anderson/dev/cohezion/research/challenges/luma_amd_speedrun/autoresearch/ksearch_tree.py",
  "old_string": "    # --- Persistence ---\n\n    def save(self, path: Path) -> None:",
  "new_string": "    # --- World Model Evolution (K-Search pi_plan) ---\n\n    def get_trajectory(self, node_id: str) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:\n        \"\"\"Get full attempt history for a node and its ancestors.\"\"\"\n        trajectory: list[dict[str, Any]] = []\n        current_id: str | None = node_id\n        while current_id and current_id in self.nodes:\n            node = self.nodes[current_id]\n            trajectory.append({\n                \"node_id\": node.id,\n                \"strategy\": node.strategy,\n                \"attempts\": node.attempts,\n                \"best_us\": node.best_result_us,\n                \"status\": node.status,\n                \"history\": node.attempt_history[-5:],  # Last 5 for brevity\n            })\n            current_id = node.parent_id\n        trajectory.reverse()  # Root first\n        return trajectory\n\n    def to_summary(self, max_nodes: int = 20) -> str:\n        \"\"\"Compact tree summary for LLM prompts (token-efficient).\"\"\"\n        lines = [f\"K-Search Tree: {self.kernel_name}\"]\n        stats = self.get_stats()\n        lines.append(\n            f\"  {stats['total_nodes']} nodes \"\n            f\"({stats['active']} active, {stats['exhausted']} exhausted, \"\n            f\"{stats['pruned']} pruned), \"\n            f\"best={stats['best_us']}µs\"\n        )\n        # Show nodes sorted by priority (most promising first)\n        sorted_nodes = sorted(\n            self.nodes.values(),\n            key=lambda n: (n.status != \"active\", -n.priority),\n        )\n        for node in sorted_nodes[:max_nodes]:\n            depth = self._depth(node.id)\n            indent = \"  \" * (depth + 1)\n            result = f\"{node.best_result_us:.1f}µs\" if node.best_result_us else \"untested\"\n            lines.append(\n                f\"{indent}[{node.status[0].upper()}] {node.strategy} \"\n                f\"(p={node.priority:.2f}, {result}, \"\n                f\"{node.attempts} attempts)\"\n            )\n        return \"\\n\".join(lines)\n\n    def _depth(self, node_id: str) -> int:\n        \"\"\"Compute depth of a node in the tree.\"\"\"\n        depth = 0\n        current = node_id\n        while current in self.nodes and self.nodes[current].parent_id:\n            current = self.nodes[current].parent_id  # type: ignore[assignment]\n            depth += 1\n        return depth\n\n    def apply_evolution(self, evolution: dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, int]:\n        \"\"\"Atomically apply INSERT/UPDATE/PRUNE from LLM world model.\n\n        Args:\n            evolution: {\"insert\": [...], \"update\": {...}, \"prune\": [...]}\n\n        Returns:\n            Counts: {\"inserted\": N, \"updated\": N, \"pruned\": N}\n        \"\"\"\n        counts = {\"inserted\": 0, \"updated\": 0, \"pruned\": 0}\n\n        # INSERT new child nodes\n        for child in evolution.get(\"insert\", []):\n            parent_id = child.get(\"parent_id\", self.root_id)\n            if parent_id and parent_id in self.nodes:\n                strategy = child.get(\"strategy\", \"\")\n                if not strategy:\n                    continue\n                priority = float(child.get(\"priority\", 0.6))\n                params = child.get(\"parameters\", {})\n                try:\n                    self.insert_child(\n                        parent_id=parent_id,\n                        strategy=strategy,\n                        parameters=params,\n                        priority=min(1.0, max(0.0, priority)),\n                        notes=\"[LLM-proposed]\",\n                    )\n                    counts[\"inserted\"] += 1\n                    log.info(f\"LLM INSERT: '{strategy}' under {parent_id}\")\n                except (KeyError, ValueError) as e:\n                    log.warning(f\"LLM INSERT failed: {e}\")\n\n        # UPDATE V-scores (priorities)\n        for node_id, new_priority in evolution.get(\"update\", {}).items():\n            if node_id in self.nodes:\n                node = self.nodes[node_id]\n                old_p = node.priority\n                node.priority = min(1.0, max(0.0, float(new_priority)))\n                counts[\"updated\"] += 1\n                log.info(\n                    f\"LLM UPDATE: {node_id} priority {old_p:.2f} → {node.priority:.2f}\"\n                )\n\n        # PRUNE branches\n        for node_id in evolution.get(\"prune\", []):\n            if node_id in self.nodes and self.nodes[node_id].status == \"active\":\n                self.strategic_prune(node_id, reason=\"LLM world model\")\n                counts[\"pruned\"] += 1\n\n        return counts\n\n    def strategic_prune(self, node_id: str, reason: str = \"\") -> None:\n        \"\"\"Prune a node and all its active descendants.\"\"\"\n        if node_id not in self.nodes:\n            return\n        node = self.nodes[node_id]\n        node.status = \"pruned\"\n        node.notes += f\" [STRATEGIC PRUNE: {reason}]\"\n        log.info(f\"PRUNE: {node_id} ({node.strategy}) — {reason}\")\n        # Recursively prune children\n        for child_id in node.children:\n            if child_id in self.nodes and self.nodes[child_id].status == \"active\":\n                self.strategic_prune(child_id, reason=f\"parent {node_id} pruned\")\n\n    def update_v_scores(self, scores: dict[str, float]) -> None:\n        \"\"\"Bulk update node priorities (V-scores) from LLM assessment.\"\"\"\n        for node_id, score in scores.items():\n            if node_id in self.nodes:\n                self.nodes[node_id].priority = min(1.0, max(0.0, score))\n\n    # --- Persistence ---\n\n    def save(self, path: Path) -> None:"
}
```

`★ Insight ─────────────────────────────────────`
**K-Search pi_code separation**: The key design choice here is making `synthesize_kernel()` a **peer** to `generate_submission()`, not a replacement. If the LLM generates bad code (syntax error, correctness failure), we fall back to templates. This means the system can only get *better* with LLM — never worse. The K-Search paper calls this "K=7 stagnation" on the code level: if the LLM can't produce passing code in 7 tries for a given strategy, it's a strategy problem, not a code problem.

**Ollama choice**: Using local `qwen3-coder:30b` via HTTP API means zero cost, no rate limits, and ~10-15s latency per call. The 4096 token cap keeps each call cheap. Falling back gracefully if Ollama is down preserves the existing deterministic behavior.
`─────────────────────────────────────────────────`

```
     1→"""Popcorn-cli wrapper for kernel evaluation.
     2→
     3→Handles test/benchmark/leaderboard submission modes,
     4→output parsing for per-shape timings, and retry logic.
     5→"""
     6→
     7→from __future__ import annotations
     8→
     9→import json
    10→import math
    11→import re
    12→import subprocess
    13→import time
    14→from dataclasses import dataclass, field
    15→from pathlib import Path
    16→from typing import Any
    17→
    18→
    19→POPCORN_CLI = Path.home() / ".local" / "bin" / "popcorn-cli"
    20→TIMEOUT_SECONDS = 720  # 12 min cap
    21→
    22→# Leaderboard names from popcorn-cli skill
    23→LEADERBOARD_NAMES = {
    24→    "moe": "amd-moe-mxfp4",
    25→    "gemm": "amd-mxfp4-mm",
    26→    "mla": "amd-mixed-mla",
    27→}
    28→
    29→# Kernel directory names (for submission path)
    30→KERNEL_DIRS = {
    31→    "moe": "moe-mxfp4",
    32→    "gemm": "mxfp4-mm",
    33→    "mla": "mixed-mla",
    34→}
    35→
    36→
    37→@dataclass
    38→class EvalResult:
    39→    """Result of a popcorn-cli evaluation."""
    40→    kernel: str
    41→    mode: str  # test | benchmark | leaderboard
    42→    success: bool
    43→    geomean_us: float | None = None
    44→    per_shape_us: dict[str, float] = field(default_factory=dict)
    45→    raw_output: str = ""
    46→    error: str = ""
    47→    duration_s: float = 0.0
    48→    timestamp: float = field(default_factory=time.time)
    49→
    50→    def to_dict(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
    51→        return {
    52→            "kernel": self.kernel,
    53→            "mode": self.mode,
    54→            "success": self.success,
    55→            "geomean_us": self.geomean_us,
    56→            "per_shape_us": self.per_shape_us,
    57→            "error": self.error,
    58→            "duration_s": self.duration_s,
    59→            "timestamp": self.timestamp,
    60→        }
    61→
    62→
    63→def _parse_benchmark_output(output: str) -> tuple[float | None, dict[str, float]]:
    64→    """Parse popcorn-cli benchmark output for timings.
    65→
    66→    Popcorn-cli benchmark format (proven from autokernel.py):
    67→        ⏱ 19.0 ± 0.02 µs
    68→        ⚡ 18.0 µs 🐌 23.8 µs
    69→
    70→    Tries multiple strategies in order:
    71→    1. Explicit "geomean" or "geometric mean" line
    72→    2. ⏱ timer emoji lines (popcorn-cli per-shape medians)
    73→    3. "median:" style lines
    74→    4. Tabular format fallback
    75→
    76→    Returns (geomean_us, {shape_key: time_us})
    77→    """
    78→    per_shape: dict[str, float] = {}
    79→    geomean: float | None = None
    80→
    81→    # Strategy 1: Explicit geomean line
    82→    for line in output.split("\n"):
    83→        lower = line.lower()
    84→        if "geomean" in lower or "geometric" in lower:
    85→            match = re.search(r"(\d+\.?\d*)\s*(?:us|µs|microsec)", line, re.IGNORECASE)
    86→            if match:
    87→                geomean = float(match.group(1))
    88→                break
    89→            match = re.search(r":\s*(\d+\.?\d*)", line)
    90→            if match:
    91→                geomean = float(match.group(1))
    92→                break
    93→
    94→    # Strategy 2: Parse popcorn-cli timer emoji lines: "⏱ 19.0 ± 0.02 µs"
    95→    medians: list[float] = []
    96→    for line in output.split("\n"):
    97→        match = re.search(r"⏱\s*(\d+\.?\d*)\s*±", line)
    98→        if match:
    99→            medians.append(float(match.group(1)))
   100→            per_shape[f"shape_{len(per_shape)}"] = float(match.group(1))
   101→
   102→    if medians and geomean is None:
   103→        log_sum = sum(math.log(m) for m in medians)
   104→        geomean = round(math.exp(log_sum / len(medians)), 2)
   105→
   106→    # Strategy 3: Parse "median:" style lines
   107→    if not medians:
   108→        for line in output.split("\n"):
   109→            lower = line.lower()
   110→            if "median" in lower:
   111→                match = re.search(r"median[:\s=]+(\d+\.?\d*)\s*(?:us|µs)?", lower)
   112→                if match:
   113→                    medians.append(float(match.group(1)))
   114→                    per_shape[f"shape_{len(per_shape)}"] = float(match.group(1))
   115→
   116→        if medians and geomean is None:
   117→            log_sum = sum(math.log(m) for m in medians)
   118→            geomean = round(math.exp(log_sum / len(medians)), 2)
   119→
   120→    # Strategy 4: Tabular format fallback: "  4  2880   512   8.198"
   121→    if not medians:
   122→        for line in output.split("\n"):
   123→            tab_match = re.search(r"^\s*(\d+)\s+(\d+)\s+(\d+)\s+([0-9.]+)\s*$", line)
   124→            if tab_match:
   125→                shape_key = f"{tab_match.group(1)}_{tab_match.group(2)}_{tab_match.group(3)}"
   126→                per_shape[shape_key] = float(tab_match.group(4))
   127→
   128→    return geomean, per_shape
   129→
   130→
   131→def _run_popcorn(
   132→    submission_path: Path,
   133→    kernel: str,
   134→    mode: str,
   135→    timeout: int = TIMEOUT_SECONDS,
   136→) -> subprocess.CompletedProcess:
   137→    """Run popcorn-cli submit command."""
   138→    leaderboard = LEADERBOARD_NAMES[kernel]
   139→    cmd = [
   140→        str(POPCORN_CLI), "submit",
   141→        "--no-tui",
   142→        "--mode", mode,
   143→        "--gpu", "MI355X",
   144→        "--leaderboard", leaderboard,
   145→        str(submission_path),
   146→    ]
   147→    return subprocess.run(
   148→        cmd,
   149→        capture_output=True,
   150→        text=True,
   151→        timeout=timeout,
   152→    )
   153→
   154→
   155→def evaluate(
   156→    submission_path: Path,
   157→    kernel: str,
   158→    mode: str = "test",
   159→    retry_on_artifact_failure: bool = True,
   160→) -> EvalResult:
   161→    """Submit a kernel to popcorn-cli and parse results.
   162→
   163→    Args:
   164→        submission_path: Path to the submission.py file
   165→        kernel: "moe", "gemm", or "mla"
   166→        mode: "test", "benchmark", or "leaderboard"
   167→        retry_on_artifact_failure: Retry once on transient artifact errors
   168→
   169→    Returns:
   170→        EvalResult with parsed timings or error info
   171→    """
   172→    if kernel not in LEADERBOARD_NAMES:
   173→        raise ValueError(f"Unknown kernel: {kernel}")
   174→
   175→    start = time.time()
   176→
   177→    try:
   178→        result = _run_popcorn(submission_path, kernel, mode)
   179→        output = result.stdout + "\n" + result.stderr
   180→        duration = time.time() - start
   181→
   182→        # Check for transient artifact failures
   183→        if retry_on_artifact_failure and "artifact" in output.lower() and result.returncode != 0:
   184→            time.sleep(5)
   185→            result = _run_popcorn(submission_path, kernel, mode)
   186→            output = result.stdout + "\n" + result.stderr
   187→            duration = time.time() - start
   188→
   189→        if result.returncode != 0:
   190→            # Check for correctness errors
   191→            if "mismatch" in output.lower() or "incorrect" in output.lower():
   192→                return EvalResult(
   193→                    kernel=kernel, mode=mode, success=False,
   194→                    raw_output=output, error="Correctness check failed",
   195→                    duration_s=duration,
   196→                )
   197→            return EvalResult(
   198→                kernel=kernel, mode=mode, success=False,
   199→                raw_output=output, error=f"Exit code {result.returncode}",
   200→                duration_s=duration,
   201→            )
   202→
   203→        geomean, per_shape = _parse_benchmark_output(output)
   204→        return EvalResult(
   205→            kernel=kernel, mode=mode, success=True,
   206→            geomean_us=geomean, per_shape_us=per_shape,
   207→            raw_output=output, duration_s=duration,
   208→        )
   209→
   210→    except subprocess.TimeoutExpired:
   211→        return EvalResult(
   212→            kernel=kernel, mode=mode, success=False,
   213→            error=f"Timeout after {TIMEOUT_SECONDS}s",
   214→            duration_s=time.time() - start,
   215→        )
   216→    except Exception as e:
   217→        return EvalResult(
   218→            kernel=kernel, mode=mode, success=False,
   219→            error=str(e),
   220→            duration_s=time.time() - start,
   221→        )
   222→
   223→
   224→def test_then_benchmark(
   225→    submission_path: Path,
   226→    kernel: str,
   227→) -> EvalResult:
   228→    """Run test first, then benchmark if test passes."""
   229→    test_result = evaluate(submission_path, kernel, mode="test")
   230→    if not test_result.success:
   231→        return test_result
   232→    return evaluate(submission_path, kernel, mode="benchmark")
   233→
```

```json
{
  "file_path": "/home/mike-anderson/dev/cohezion/research/challenges/luma_amd_speedrun/autoresearch/code_synthesizer.py",
  "content": "\"\"\"LLM-based kernel code synthesis (K-Search pi_code).\n\nGenerates submission.py implementations using local Ollama (qwen3-coder:30b).\nFalls back to None if Ollama is unavailable or generates invalid code.\nSeparates strategy failure from implementation failure via repeated sampling.\n\"\"\"\n\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport ast\nimport json\nimport logging\nimport re\nimport time\nfrom pathlib import Path\nfrom typing import Any\nfrom urllib.error import URLError\nfrom urllib.request import Request, urlopen\n\nlog = logging.getLogger(\"code_synthesizer\")\n\nOLLAMA_URL = \"http://localhost:11434/api/generate\"\nOLLAMA_MODEL = \"qwen3-coder:30b\"\nOLLAMA_TIMEOUT = 60  # seconds\nMAX_TOKENS = 4096\n\nBASE_DIR = Path(__file__).parent\nKERNELS_DIR = BASE_DIR.parent / \"kernels\"\n\n# Kernel directory names matching evaluator.py\nKERNEL_DIRS = {\n    \"moe\": \"moe-mxfp4\",\n    \"gemm\": \"mxfp4-mm\",\n    \"mla\": \"mixed-mla\",\n}\n\n\ndef _read_reference_code(kernel: str) -> str:\n    \"\"\"Read the reference.py for a kernel as context for the LLM.\"\"\"\n    ref_path = KERNELS_DIR / KERNEL_DIRS.get(kernel, kernel) / \"reference.py\"\n    if ref_path.exists():\n        return ref_path.read_text()[:3000]  # Truncate for token budget\n    return \"\"\n\n\ndef _read_research_strategy() -> str:\n    \"\"\"Read the human-editable research strategy file.\"\"\"\n    strategy_path = BASE_DIR / \"research_strategy.md\"\n    if strategy_path.exists():\n        return strategy_path.read_text()[:1500]  # Keep it short\n    return \"\"\n\n\ndef _read_dead_ends(kernel: str) -> list[str]:\n    \"\"\"Extract dead ends from research strategy.\"\"\"\n    strategy = _read_research_strategy()\n    dead_ends: list[str] = []\n    in_dead_ends = False\n    for line in strategy.split(\"\\n\"):\n        if \"dead end\" in line.lower() or \"do not retry\" in line.lower():\n            in_dead_ends = True\n            continue\n        if in_dead_ends:\n            if line.startswith(\"##\"):\n                break\n            stripped = line.strip(\"- \").strip()\n            if stripped:\n                dead_ends.append(stripped)\n    return dead_ends\n\n\ndef _call_ollama(prompt: str, model: str = OLLAMA_MODEL) -> str | None:\n    \"\"\"Call local Ollama API. Returns response text or None on failure.\"\"\"\n    payload = json.dumps({\n        \"model\": model,\n        \"prompt\": prompt,\n        \"stream\": False,\n        \"options\": {\n            \"num_predict\": MAX_TOKENS,\n            \"temperature\": 0.7,\n        },\n    }).encode()\n\n    req = Request(\n        OLLAMA_URL,\n        data=payload,\n        headers={\"Content-Type\": \"application/json\"},\n        method=\"POST\",\n    )\n\n    try:\n        with urlopen(req, timeout=OLLAMA_TIMEOUT) as resp:\n            data = json.loads(resp.read())\n            return data.get(\"response\", \"\")\n    except (URLError, TimeoutError, json.JSONDecodeError, OSError) as e:\n        log.warning(f\"Ollama call failed: {e}\")\n        return None\n\n\ndef _extract_python_code(response: str) -> str | None:\n    \"\"\"Extract Python code from LLM response (handles markdown fences).\"\"\"\n    # Try to extract from ```python ... ``` blocks\n    pattern = r\"```python\\s*\\n(.*?)```\"\n    matches = re.findall(pattern, response, re.DOTALL)\n    if matches:\n        return matches[0].strip()\n\n    # Try ``` ... ``` blocks\n    pattern = r\"```\\s*\\n(.*?)```\"\n    matches = re.findall(pattern, response, re.DOTALL)\n    if matches:\n        return matches[0].strip()\n\n    # If response looks like raw Python (starts with import/from/def)\n    lines = response.strip().split(\"\\n\")\n    if lines and any(lines[0].startswith(kw) for kw in (\"import \", \"from \", \"def \", \"#\")):\n        return response.strip()\n\n    return None\n\n\ndef _validate_submission(code: str) -> bool:\n    \"\"\"Validate generated submission.py has required structure.\"\"\"\n    try:\n        ast.parse(code)\n    except SyntaxError:\n        return False\n\n    # Must define custom_kernel\n    if \"custom_kernel\" not in code and \"def custom_kernel\" not in code:\n        return False\n\n    return True\n\n\ndef synthesize_kernel(\n    strategy: str,\n    kernel: str,\n    trajectory: list[dict[str, Any]] | None = None,\n    constraints: list[str] | None = None,\n) -> str | None:\n    \"\"\"Generate a submission.py using LLM code synthesis.\n\n    Args:\n        strategy: High-level optimization strategy description\n        kernel: \"moe\", \"gemm\", or \"mla\"\n        trajectory: Past attempts on this strategy branch\n        constraints: Known dead ends and limitations\n\n    Returns:\n        Generated Python code string, or None if synthesis fails\n    \"\"\"\n    reference_code = _read_reference_code(kernel)\n    dead_ends = constraints or _read_dead_ends(kernel)\n    research_strategy = _read_research_strategy()\n\n    # Build trajectory context\n    trajectory_text = \"\"\n    if trajectory:\n        recent = trajectory[-3:]  # Last 3 entries\n        for t in recent:\n            history_summary = \"\"\n            for h in t.get(\"history\", [])[-2:]:\n                history_summary += f\"  - {h['result_us']:.1f}µs ({h.get('source', '?')})\\n\"\n            trajectory_text += (\n                f\"- {t['strategy']}: best={t.get('best_us', '?')}µs, \"\n                f\"{t['attempts']} attempts, status={t['status']}\\n\"\n                f\"{history_summary}\"\n            )\n\n    dead_ends_text = \"\\n\".join(f\"- {d}\" for d in dead_ends) if dead_ends else \"None known\"\n\n    prompt = f\"\"\"Generate a submission.py for an AMD MI355X (gfx950) {kernel.upper()} kernel optimization.\n\n## Strategy\n{strategy}\n\n## Reference Implementation\n```python\n{reference_code}\n```\n\n## Research Direction\n{research_strategy[:800] if research_strategy else 'No research strategy file.'}\n\n## Previous Attempts on This Branch\n{trajectory_text if trajectory_text else 'First attempt on this strategy.'}\n\n## Dead Ends (Do NOT use these approaches)\n{dead_ends_text}\n\n## Requirements\n1. Define `custom_kernel(data: input_t) -> output_t` matching the reference signature\n2. Must pass correctness check within rtol=1e-2\n3. Must be faster than baseline\n4. Use only libraries available on the MI355X runner (torch, triton, aiter)\n\n## Output\nOutput ONLY the complete Python code for submission.py. No explanation.\"\"\"\n\n    start = time.time()\n    response = _call_ollama(prompt)\n    elapsed = time.time() - start\n\n    if response is None:\n        log.info(\"LLM unavailable, skipping code synthesis\")\n        return None\n\n    log.info(f\"LLM response in {elapsed:.1f}s ({len(response)} chars)\")\n\n    code = _extract_python_code(response)\n    if code is None:\n        log.warning(\"Could not extract Python code from LLM response\")\n        return None\n\n    if not _validate_submission(code):\n        log.warning(\"LLM-generated code failed validation\")\n        return None\n\n    log.info(f\"LLM synthesized valid submission ({len(code)} chars)\")\n    return code\n\n\ndef is_ollama_available(model: str = OLLAMA_MODEL) -> bool:\n    \"\"\"Check if Ollama is running and the model is available.\"\"\"\n    try:\n        req = Request(\n            \"http://localhost:11434/api/tags\",\n            method=\"GET\",\n        )\n        with urlopen(req, timeout=5) as resp:\n            data = json.loads(resp.read())\n            models = [m.get(\"name\", \"\") for m in data.get(\"models\", [])]\n            # Check for model name (with or without tag)\n            return any(model.split(\":\")[0] in m for m in models)\n    except (URLError, TimeoutError, json.JSONDecodeError, OSError):\n        return False\n"
}
```

`★ Insight ─────────────────────────────────────`
**World model co-evolution** is the K-Search paper's biggest contribution. Instead of the tree being static (pre-seeded at start), the LLM acts as a "world model" that learns from each result and proposes new strategies. The LLM sees: (1) the current tree structure, (2) the trajectory of the node that was just tested, and (3) the research strategy. It outputs structured INSERT/UPDATE/PRUNE operations that reshape the search space. This is fundamentally different from random parameter perturbation — it's guided exploration.
`─────────────────────────────────────────────────`

```json
{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/home/mike-anderson/dev/cohezion/research/challenges/luma_amd_speedrun/autoresearch/analyzer.py",
  "old_string": "def get_best_result(log_path: Path) -> dict[str, Any] | None:\n    \"\"\"Get the best result (lowest geomean) from log.\"\"\"\n    results = load_results(log_path)\n    successful = [r for r in results if r.get(\"success\") and r.get(\"geomean_us\")]\n    if not successful:\n        return None\n    return min(successful, key=lambda r: r[\"geomean_us\"])",
  "new_string": "def get_best_result(log_path: Path) -> dict[str, Any] | None:\n    \"\"\"Get the best result (lowest geomean) from log.\"\"\"\n    results = load_results(log_path)\n    successful = [r for r in results if r.get(\"success\") and r.get(\"geomean_us\")]\n    if not successful:\n        return None\n    return min(successful, key=lambda r: r[\"geomean_us\"])\n\n\ndef evolve_world_model(\n    tree: KSearchTree,\n    node: KNode,\n    result: EvalResult,\n    analysis: Analysis,\n) -> dict[str, int]:\n    \"\"\"K-Search Phase 3: World model co-evolution via LLM.\n\n    After analyzing a result, ask the LLM to propose tree mutations:\n    - INSERT: New child strategies worth exploring\n    - UPDATE: Revised V-scores for affected nodes\n    - PRUNE: Branches that should be abandoned\n\n    Returns counts of operations applied, or empty dict if LLM unavailable.\n    \"\"\"\n    from code_synthesizer import _call_ollama, _read_research_strategy\n\n    trajectory = tree.get_trajectory(node.id)\n    tree_summary = tree.to_summary(max_nodes=15)\n    research_strategy = _read_research_strategy()\n\n    geomean = result.geomean_us or float(\"inf\")\n    result_summary = (\n        f\"Strategy: {node.strategy}\\n\"\n        f\"Result: {geomean:.1f}µs \"\n        f\"(improvement: {analysis.improvement_pct:+.1f}%)\\n\"\n        f\"Bottleneck: {analysis.bottleneck_shape or 'N/A'}\\n\"\n        f\"Suggestion: {analysis.suggestion}\"\n    )\n\n    # Format trajectory\n    traj_text = \"\"\n    for t in trajectory[-4:]:\n        traj_text += (\n            f\"  {t['node_id']}: {t['strategy']} \"\n            f\"(best={t.get('best_us', '?')}µs, {t['attempts']} attempts)\\n\"\n        )\n\n    prompt = f\"\"\"You are a GPU kernel optimization world model for AMD MI355X.\n\n## Current Tree\n{tree_summary}\n\n## Last Result\n{result_summary}\n\n## Trajectory (root → current)\n{traj_text}\n\n## Research Strategy\n{research_strategy[:600] if research_strategy else 'None'}\n\nBased on this result, propose tree mutations. Be conservative — only insert\nstrategies that are meaningfully different from existing nodes.\n\nOutput valid JSON only:\n{{\n  \"insert\": [\n    {{\"parent_id\": \"node_id\", \"strategy\": \"description\", \"priority\": 0.6, \"parameters\": {{}}}}\n  ],\n  \"update\": {{\n    \"node_id\": 0.7\n  }},\n  \"prune\": [\"node_id_to_prune\"]\n}}\n\nRules:\n- INSERT 0-2 children max (quality over quantity)\n- UPDATE only nodes where the result changes your confidence\n- PRUNE only clearly dead-end branches\n- Use existing node IDs from the tree summary\n- Output ONLY the JSON, no explanation\"\"\"\n\n    response = _call_ollama(prompt)\n    if response is None:\n        log.info(\"LLM unavailable for world model evolution\")\n        return {}\n\n    # Parse JSON from response\n    try:\n        # Strip markdown fences if present\n        clean = response.strip()\n        if clean.startswith(\"```\"):\n            lines = clean.split(\"\\n\")\n            clean = \"\\n\".join(lines[1:-1] if lines[-1].strip() == \"```\" else lines[1:])\n        evolution = json.loads(clean)\n    except (json.JSONDecodeError, ValueError) as e:\n        log.warning(f\"Failed to parse LLM evolution response: {e}\")\n        return {}\n\n    if not isinstance(evolution, dict):\n        log.warning(\"LLM returned non-dict evolution response\")\n        return {}\n\n    counts = tree.apply_evolution(evolution)\n    log.info(\n        f\"World model evolution: \"\n        f\"+{counts['inserted']} inserted, \"\n        f\"~{counts['updated']} updated, \"\n        f\"-{counts['pruned']} pruned\"\n    )\n    return counts"
}
```

`★ Insight ─────────────────────────────────────`
**Karpathy's `program.md` pattern**: The key idea is a human-editable file that the autonomous loop reads before each cycle. This creates a "steering wheel" — the loop runs overnight autonomously, but you can adjust its direction by editing this file. Dead ends are especially valuable: they prevent the LLM from wasting cycles rediscovering paths you already know are blocked.
`─────────────────────────────────────────────────`

```json
{
  "file_path": "/home/mike-anderson/dev/cohezion/research/challenges/luma_amd_speedrun/autoresearch/research_strategy.md",
  "content": "# Research Strategy (Human-Editable)\n\nThis file guides the LLM world model's optimization direction.\nEdit priorities and dead ends to steer overnight runs.\nThe autoresearch loop reads this before each LLM call.\n\n## Current Focus\n\n- **MoE** (50% budget): Try direct `moe_sorting_fwd` with `local_expert_mask` parameter.\n  Explore `fmoe_g1u1` for 256-expert shapes (NaN for 32-expert — do NOT try there).\n  KSPLIT adaptive strategy: KSPLIT=4 for sparse (>200 experts), KSPLIT=2 otherwise.\n- **GEMM** (30% budget): Explore `tritonblas.matmul_fp4` as alternative to `gemm_a4w4`.\n  Fused quant+GEMM is the only path below API ceiling (~33µs).\n  All tensors MUST be `torch.uint8` views for tritonblas. B layout is [N, K//2] row-major.\n- **MLA** (20% budget): At Python dispatch ceiling (~67.8µs vs leader 4.3µs, 15.8x gap).\n  Deprioritize unless custom Triton/CK kernel path opens up.\n  Three-regime routing (matmul + aiter a16w8 + aiter a8w8) with `fast_mode=False` is optimal.\n\n## Dead Ends (Do NOT retry)\n\n- `gemm_afp4wfp4`: KeyError 'float4_e2m1fn_x2' — MXFP4 not supported in this API\n- `mla_decode_fwd` with MXFP4 KV cache: \"only support head_size == KV.size(3) for now\"\n- `get_torch_quant` as drop-in for `get_triton_quant`: produces wrong GEMM results\n- `fmoe_g1u1` for 32-expert shapes: produces NaN\n- `torch.compile` on ROCm 7.1: blocked by `auto_functionalized_v2`\n- Custom HIP compilation: blocked by runner source scanning\n- A-quantization caching across iterations: only helps benchmark mode, NOT ranked scoring\n- `fast_mode=True` for MLA metadata: SLOWER on MI355X (verified Phase 17)\n- Origami XCD remapping for non-divisible tiles: creates non-bijective mapping (silent wrong results)\n\n## Leaderboard Gaps (Updated)\n\n| Kernel | Our Best | Leader | Gap | Path Forward |\n|--------|----------|--------|-----|--------------|\n| MoE | ~155µs | 145µs | 1.07x | fused_moe tuning, direct CK dispatch |\n| GEMM | ~33µs | ~23µs | 1.45x | Fused quant+GEMM (requires custom Triton) |\n| MLA | ~67.8µs | 4.3µs | 15.8x | Single fused CK/ASM kernel (moonshot) |\n\n## Exploration Priorities\n\n1. Any strategy that fuses quantization into the GEMM kernel\n2. MoE: direct `ck_moe_stage1`/`ck_moe_stage2` calling conventions\n3. GEMM: Triton `tl.dot_scaled` with correct scale layout [BLOCK_N, SCALE_PER_K]\n4. MLA: FlashAttention-style fused tiling (eliminates 3-stage pipeline overhead)\n"
}
```

`★ Insight ─────────────────────────────────────`
**R-Zero Challenger pattern** is the simplest of the three integrations. Instead of the complex Challenger-Solver co-evolution from the paper (which trains adversarial problem generators), we just raise the target by 10% when convergence is detected. This "move the goalpost" pattern prevents the optimizer from stopping at a local optimum. It's a 5-line addition that re-activates the search when it would otherwise terminate.
`─────────────────────────────────────────────────`

> AGENT

`★ Insight ─────────────────────────────────────`
**Layered fallback**: The generator now has a 3-tier code generation strategy: (1) LLM synthesis via `code_synthesizer.py` — tries to generate novel code from the strategy description, (2) Template generation — the existing deterministic approach using `string.Template`, (3) If both fail, the cycle reports failure and moves on. This means adding LLM is purely additive — it can only improve results, never degrade them.
`─────────────────────────────────────────────────`

Expected: {list(TEMPLATES)}\")\n\n    # Merge defaults with overrides\n    params = {**DEFAULTS[kernel], **parameters}",
```json
  "new_string": "def generate_submission(\n    kernel: str,\n    parameters: dict[str, Any],\n    output_path: Path | None = None,\n    strategy: str = \"\",\n    trajectory: list[dict[str, Any]] | None = None,\n    use_llm: bool = True,\n) -> str:\n    \"\"\"Generate a submission.py, trying LLM synthesis first then templates.\n\n    Args:\n        kernel: \"moe\", \"gemm\", or \"mla\"\n        parameters: Overrides for template defaults\n        output_path: If provided, write the generated code to this file\n        strategy: Strategy description for LLM synthesis\n        trajectory: Past attempts for LLM context\n        use_llm: Whether to attempt LLM synthesis before templates\n\n    Returns:\n        Generated Python source code\n    \"\"\"\n    # Tier 1: Try LLM synthesis if strategy is provided\n    if use_llm and strategy:\n        try:\n            from code_synthesizer import synthesize_kernel\n            code = synthesize_kernel(\n                strategy=strategy,\n                kernel=kernel,\n                trajectory=trajectory,\n            )\n            if code:\n                log.info(f\"Using LLM-synthesized code for {kernel}\")\n                if output_path:\n                    output_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n                    output_path.write_text(code)\n                return code\n            log.info(f\"LLM synthesis returned None, falling back to template\")\n        except Exception as e:\n            log.warning(f\"LLM synthesis failed, falling back to template: {e}\")\n\n    # Tier 2: Template-based generation\n    if kernel not in TEMPLATES:\n        raise ValueError(f\"Unknown kernel: {kernel}. Expected: {list(TEMPLATES)}\")\n\n    # Merge defaults with overrides\n    params = {**DEFAULTS[kernel], **parameters}"
}
```
